{"forms":[{"form":"dogholes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A place regarded as fit only for dogs: a horrid, mean habitation."],"tags":["derogatory","idiomatic"]},{"glosses":["A small, shallow bay or inlet, usually surrounded by high cliffs, that is accessible only by smaller boats."]},{"glosses":["A type of small schooner designed in the 19th century to navigate in shallow waters and to conduct coastal shipping in and out of doghole ports."]},{"glosses":["A mine worked by fewer than fifteen miners, which is small enough that some safety laws do not apply."],"tags":["slang"],"topics":["business","mining"]},{"glosses":["A mine worked by fewer than fifteen miners, which is small enough that some safety laws do not apply.","Such a small mine that is dug independently by one or a few miners, often clandestinely and illegally: a bootleg mine."],"tags":["slang"],"topics":["business","mining"]},{"glosses":["An excavated area that acts as an access hole or that connects different parts of a mine."],"topics":["business","mining"]},{"glosses":["A tiny, uncomfortable hole or cell, usually too small to stand in, in which prisoners are confined as punishment."]},{"glosses":["An underground bolthole dug to hide from enemy soldiers."]},{"glosses":["One of the entrances to a system of prairie dog tunnels."]},{"glosses":["A hole that was dug by a dog."]},{"glosses":["A hole drilled for the placement of a bench dog."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈdɒɡhəʊl/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈdɔɡˌhoʊl/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈdɑɡ-/","tags":["General-American","cot-caught-merger"]}],"upstream_line":3952370,"upstream_offset":9771238028,"upstream_record_sha256":"4d56860643af65ea9538bd16f0552916225118c0d1c6c9dc30247a8d85525845","word":"doghole"}
